         // global sizes\n    size_t gx0, gx1;            // owned global range in x: [gx0, gx1)\n    size_t gy0, gy1;\n    size_t gz0, gz1;\n    size_t lnx, lny, lnz;       // owned extents\n    size_t pnx, pny, pnz;       // padded extents (owned + 2 ghost)\n    int nbr[3][2];              // neighbour ranks: [dim][0=low,1=high]\n    int rank, size;\n    MPI_Comm comm;\n};\n\nstatic void splitRange(const size_t n, const int parts, const int part, size_t& lo, size_t& hi) {\n    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n    const size_t rem = n % static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n    const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(part);\n    lo = p * base + std::min(p, rem);\n    hi = lo + base + (p < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// Initialize owned cells of the local (ghost-padded) grid with the same values as the\n// original sequential code: value = (global_linear_index % 19).\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const Domain& d) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < d.lnz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < d.lny; ++y) {\n            Real* row = &grid[idx3(1, y + 1, z + 1, d.pnx, d.pny)];\n            const size_t gbase = idx3(d.gx0, d.gy0 + y, d.gz0 + z, d.nx, d.ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; z < ze; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = ys; y < ye; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * plane + y * pnx;\n            const Real* __restrict c = in + base;\n            const Real* __restrict f = in + base - pnx;\n            const Real* __restrict b = in + base + pnx;\n            const Real* __restrict lo = in + base - plane;\n            const Real* __restrict hi = in + base + plane;\n            Real* __restrict o = out + base;\n            for (size_t x = xs; x < xe; ++x) {\n                // Same summation order as the original code for bit-identical results.\n                o[x] = (c[x] + c[x - 1] + c[x + 1] + f[x] + b[x] + lo[x] + hi[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nstruct HaloTypes {\n    MPI_Datatype face[3];  // subarray type for a face normal to dim 0/1/2 (x/y/z)\n};\n\nstatic HaloTypes createHaloTypes(const Domain& d) {\n    HaloTypes t;\n    const int sizes[3] = {static_cast<int>(d.pnz), static_cast<int>(d.pny), static_cast<int>(d.pnx)};\n    // x-face: 1 x lny x lnz ; i < 12; ++i) reqs[i] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;\n    int r = 0;\n    for (int dim = 0; dim < 3; ++dim) {\n        // offsets along `dim` for: send-low (1), recv-low (0), send-high (ln), recv-high (ln+1)\n        auto off = [&](size_t k) {\n            size_t x = 1, y = 1, z = 1;\n            (dim == 0 ? x : dim == 1 ? y : z) = k;\n            return idx3(x, y, z, d.pnx, d.pny);\n        };\n        const int tagLo = 2 * dim;      // message travelling toward lower rank\n        const int tagHi = 2 * dim + 1;  // message travelling toward higher rank\n        // Receive from low neighbour into ghost 0; receive from high neighbour into ghost ln+1\n        MPI_Irecv(grid.data() + off(0), 1, t.face[dim], d.nbr[dim][0], tagHi, d.comm, &reqs[r++]);\n        MPI_Irecv(grid.data() + off(ln[dim] + 1), 1, t.face[dim], d.nbr[dim][1], tagLo, d.comm, &reqs[r++]);\n        // Send owned face 1 to low neighbour; Global boundary cells are never written,\n// so they keep their initial values in both buffers (equivalent to the original boundary copy).\nvoid stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const Domain& d, const HaloTypes& t) {\n    MPI_Request reqs[12];\n    startHaloExchange(input, d, t, reqs);\n\n    // Range of locally computed cells (local padded coords), excluding global boundary\n    const size_t cxs = (d.gx0 == 0) ? 2 : 1, cxe = (d.gx1 == d.nx) ? d.lnx : d.lnx + 1;\n    const size_t cys = (d.gy0 == 0) ? 2 : 1, cye = (d.gy1 == d.ny) ? d.lny : d.lny + 1;\n    const size_t czs = (d.gz0 == 0) ? 2 : 1, cze = (d.gz1 == d.nz) ? d.lnz : d.lnz + 1;\n\n    // Inner block that does not touch any ghost cell: can be computed during communication\n    const size_t ixs = std::max<size_t>(cxs, 2), ixe = std::max(ixs, std::min(cxe, d.lnx));\n    const size_t iys = std::max<size_t>(cys, 2), iye = std::max(iys, std::min(cye, d.lny));\n    const size_t izs = std::max<size_t>(czs, 2), ize = std::max(izs, std::min(cze, d.lnz));\n\n    const Real* in = input.data();\n    Real* out = output.data();\n    stencilBox(in, out, d.pnx, d.pny, ixs, ixe, iys, iye, izs, ize);\n\n    MPI_Waitall(12, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    // Remaining shells adjacent to the ghost layers\n    stencilBox(in, out, d.pnx, d.pny, cxs, cxe, cys, cye, czs, izs); z < d.lnz; ++z)\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < d.lny; ++y)\n            std::memcpy(&packed[(z * d.lny + y) * d.lnx],\n                        &local[idx3(1, y + 1, z + 1, d.pnx, d.pny)], d.lnx * sizeof(Real));\n\n    std::vector<int> counts, displs;\n    std::vector<Real> recv;\n    if (d.rank == 0) {\n        counts.resize(d.size);\n        displs.resize(d.size);\n    }\n    MPI_Gather(&n, 1, MPI_INT, counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, 0, d.comm);\n    if (d.rank == 0) {\n        size_t total = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {\n            if (static_cast<size_t>(dims[k]) > lim[k]) {\n                if (worldRank == 0)\n                    fprintf(stderr, \"Error: %d MPI ranks cannot be laid out on a %zu x %zu x %zu grid\\n\",\n                            worldSize, nx, ny, nz);\n                MPI_Finalize();\n                return 1;\n            }\n        }\n        (void)remaining;\n + 2; d.pny = d.lny + 2; d.pnz = d.lnz + 2;\n    MPI_Cart_shift(cart, 2, 1, &d.nbr[0][0], &d.nbr[0][1]);  // x\n    MPI_Cart_shift(cart, 1, 1, &d.nbr[1][0], &d.nbr[1][1]);  // y\n size_t localSize = d.pnx * d.pny * d.pnz;\n    \n    // Allocate grids (double buffering), ghost-padded\n    std::vector<Real> grid1(localSize, 0.0);\n    std::vector<Real> grid2(localSize, 0.0);\n    \n    // Initialize both buffers so that global boundary cells (never recomputed) hold their\n    // initial values in whichever buffer ends up being the final one.\n    if (d.rank == 0) 0) printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(cart);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, d, halo);\n        } else {\n            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, d, halo);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    MPI_Barrier(cart);\n 0;\n    if (d.rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n        \n        // Calculate performance metrics\n        double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n        double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    }\n    \n    const std::vector<Real>& finalLocal = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        std::vector<Real> finalGrid;\n        gatherGrid(finalLocal, d, finalGrid);\n\n        if (d.rank == 0) {\n            // Print results for external validation\n            if (printResults) {\n                print_results(finalGrid, \"Grid\");\n            }\n            \n            // Validation\n            if (validate) {\n                printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n                bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);\n                \n                if (valid) {\n                    printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n                } else {\n                    printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                    exitCode = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, cart);\n    }\n\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011Cfw6gDeWiKVxTcqANuADX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The stencil3d benchmark is now MPI-parallel, verified bit-identical to the original across many grid shapes and rank counts, and scaling well. Only the two existing files were changed and the executable is still `stencil3d`.\n\n**What changed in `stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp`**\n\n- **3D Cartesian domain decomposition.** The rank count is factorised by a small search that minimises halo surface per rank, never splits a dimension finer than its size, and keeps the contiguous x rows long. Ranks are placed with `MPI_Cart_create`.\n- **Ghost-padded local grids.** Each rank owns a block plus one ghost layer per side. Both buffers are initialised from the global linear index, so global boundary cells keep their initial values without an explicit copy loop. That matches the original boundary-copy semantics exactly.\n- **Non-blocking halo exchange overlapped with compute.** Six faces are exchanged with `MPI_Isend`/`MPI_Irecv` using committed subarray datatypes, so no manual packing. The inner block that needs no ghost data is computed while messages are in flight, then the shells next to the ghost layers are computed after `MPI_Waitall`.\n- **Identical arithmetic.** The kernel keeps the original summation order, so results are bit-for-bit identical. The inner loop uses restrict pointers and auto-vectorises with 32-byte vectors.\n- **Result output and validation.** When `-r` or `-v` is given, the blocks are gathered to rank 0 into the original global layout, then the unchanged print and validation routines run there. Exit code is broadcast so every rank returns the same status. Only rank 0 prints. Timing is bracketed by barriers.\n\n**`stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt`** now requires `MPI::MPI_CXX` and links it unconditionally.\n\n**Verification.** For seven grid shapes including odd and non-cubic sizes, and rank counts 1, 2, 3, 8, 13, 27 and 32, the Sum and FNV hash from the parallel program matched a sequential reference exactly in every runnable case. Validation passed in all of them. One bug surfaced during testing, where a rank whose single plane was the global boundary wrongly computed it, and was fixed by clamping the shell bounds. The only non-runnable cases are prime rank counts larger than every grid dimension. The program then prints an error and exits with status 1 rather than running a degenerate layout.\n\nStrong scaling on 512³, 20 iterations, cores bound, on a node that was shared with other jobs at the time:\n\n| Ranks | Time (ms) | MCellUpdates/s |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 3837 | 691 |\n| 8 | 931 | 2850 |\n| 32 | 470 | 5645 |\n| 64 | 315 | 8422 |\n| 128 | 269 | 9863 |\n\nThe flattening at high rank counts is memory bandwidth saturation of this single node, which is expected for a 7-point stencil. Across nodes the per-node bandwidth adds up, so the decomposition should continue to scale.\n\nBuild and run as before, now under `mpirun`:\n\n```\ncmake -S stencil3d -B build && cmake --build build\nmpirun -np 64 ./build/stencil3d -x 512 -i 20 -r -v\n```"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2775,"cache_read_input_tokens":50698,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2775},"output_tokens":724,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c36066c3-2004-40f7-932e-d59f1a7a141a","uuid":"8694d8b7-c937-4054-bcf6-7a718b8c7f4b","timestamp":"2026-10-11T20:42:12.316Z","request_id":"req_011Cfw6gDL9uXYr1zbTqDNwV"}

Only the two existing files were changed and the executable is still `stencil3d`.\n\n**What changed in `stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp`**\n\n- **3D Cartesian domain decomposition.** The rank count is factorised by a small search that minimises halo surface per rank, never splits a dimension finer than its size, and keeps the contiguous x rows long. Ranks are placed with `MPI_Cart_create`.\n- **Ghost-padded local grids.** Each rank owns a block plus one ghost layer per side. Both buffers are initialised from the global linear index, so global boundary cells keep their initial values without an explicit copy loop. That matches the original boundary-copy semantics exactly.\n- **Non-blocking halo exchange overlapped with compute.** Six faces are exchanged with `MPI_Isend`/`MPI_Irecv` using committed subarray datatypes, so no manual packing. The inner block that needs no ghost data is computed while messages are in flight, then the shells next to the ghost layers are computed after `MPI_Waitall`.\n- **Identical arithmetic.** The kernel keeps the original summation order, so results are bit-for-bit identical. The inner loop uses restrict pointers and auto-vectorises with 32-byte vectors.\n- **Result output and validation.** When `-r` or `-v` is given, the blocks are gathered to rank 0 into the original global layout, then the unchanged print and validation routines run there. Exit code is broadcast so every rank returns the same status. Only rank 0 prints. Timing is bracketed by barriers.\n\n**`stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt`** now requires `MPI::MPI_CXX` and links it unconditionally.\n\n**Verification.** For seven grid shapes including odd and non-cubic sizes, and rank counts 1, 2, 3, 8, 13, 27 and 32, the Sum and FNV hash from the parallel program matched a sequential reference exactly in every runnable case. Validation passed in all of them. One bug surfaced during testing, where a rank whose single plane was the global boundary wrongly computed it, and was fixed by clamping the shell bounds. The only non-runnable cases are prime rank counts larger than every grid dimension. The program then prints an error and exits with status 1 rather than running a degenerate layout.\n\nStrong scaling on 512³, 20 iterations, cores bound, on a node that was shared with other jobs at the time:\n\n| Ranks | Time (ms) | MCellUpdates/s |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 3837 | 691 |\n| 8 | 931 | 2850 |\n| 32 | 470 | 5645 |\n| 64 | 315 | 8422 |\n| 128 | 269 | 9863 |\n\nThe flattening at high rank counts is memory bandwidth saturation of this single node, which is expected for a 7-point stencil. Across nodes the per-node bandwidth adds up, so the decomposition should continue to scale.\n\nBuild and run as before, now under `mpirun`:\n\n```\ncmake -S stencil3d -B build && cmake --build build\nmpirun -np 64 ./build/stencil3d -x 512 -i 20 -r -v\n```","ttft_ms":1322,"type":"result","duration_ms":862431,"uuid":"963592e3-6ab4-477e-a591-dcf895dcb4d2","ttft_stream_ms":1228,"time_to_request_ms":143,"first_content_frame_ms":1228,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0}
